# Preliminary Hearing, Day 4 — Transcript (July 9, 2026)
Continues [[2026-07-06 - Preliminary Hearing Day 1 Transcript|Day 1]], [[2026-07-07 - Preliminary Hearing Day 2 Transcript|Day 2]], and [[2026-07-08 - Preliminary Hearing Day 3 Transcript|Day 3]] · *State of Utah v. Tyler James Robinson*, Case No. 251403576 · before [[Judge Tony Graf]] · video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=89ZKQIHaJpM

> 🎥 **Watch the source recording** — the timestamps below deep-link into this video.

![](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=89ZKQIHaJpM)

> Speaker labels come from automated diarization, cleaned and re-attributed by role — verify against the video before quoting a specific person by name. Witness/attorney name spellings (e.g., the firearms examiner "Ms. Karner") are our best reading of the audio.

## Summary
**What happened:** Day 4 (~8 hours — the longest session yet) continued the State's case before [[Judge Tony Graf]] and, like Days 1–3, **ended mid-hearing**, recessed to **Day 5 (July 10, 9:00 a.m.)**. The morning resolved the Day-3 **publication fight**: over defense objection the court admitted and (with redactions, to protect the jury pool) allowed the digital exhibits to be shown, and the State played **[[Lance Twiggs]]'s immunized, sworn recorded statement** — the roommate's own account, given under a **use-immunity agreement** with a false-statement admonition — during case agent **[[Brian Davis|Agent Davis]]**'s testimony; Davis was then **excused with no cross**. After lunch the State recalled **Sgt. [[Jennifer Faumuina]]**, who detailed the **searches of Robinson's townhome and his parents' home**: a **dremel tool** and bit set, two Remington **.30-06** ammunition boxes and **.30-06** cartridge cases, several long guns, a revolver, burned paper, and clothing and shoes. At **~06:14 the State RESTED its case-in-chief.** The **defense then opened its case**, calling a **firearms / tool-mark examiner (Ms. Karner)** to attack the reliability of firearm/tool-mark identification — the **AFTE** method, the **DOJ uniform-language** limits, and the **PCAST** critique — i.e., the science underneath the [[The Engraved Casings|casing]] and [[The Inconclusive Bullet|bullet]] comparisons. Throughout, Kirk-family counsel **Jeffrey Neiman** pressed to let the family view the exhibits. **No bind-over.** Full defense-side analysis: [[2026-07-09 - Preliminary Hearing Day 4|Day 4 Summary & Legal Review]].

> This is a **preliminary hearing**, not a trial — the standard is **probable cause**, and **reliable hearsay is admissible** (Utah Const. Art. I §12; Utah R. Evid. 1102). Robinson is **presumed innocent**.
